Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n        self.total_reward += reward\n        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n        \n        if not self.training:\"\n    }\n    </json>"

  38\t    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n    39\t        self.params = dict(params)\n    40\t\n    41\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    42\t        return None\n    43\t\n    44\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    45\t        return dict(self.params)\n    46\t\n    47\t\n    48\tclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    49\t    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    50\t    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    51\t    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n    52\t        self.w_start = w_start\n    53\t        self.w_end = w_end\n    54\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    55\t\n    56\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    57\t        self.it = 0\n    58\t\n    59\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    60\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    61\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    62\t        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n    63\t        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n    64\t        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    65\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    66\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n    67\t        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n    68\t        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n    69\t        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    70\t\n    71\t\n    72\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    73\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    74\t\n    75\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    76\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    77\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    78\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    79\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    80\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    81\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    82\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    83\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    84\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    85\t    \"\"\"\n    86\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    87\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    88\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    89\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    90\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    91\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    92\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    93\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    94\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    95\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    96\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    97\t\n    98\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    99\t        self.it = 0\n   100\t        self.last_gbest = None\n   101\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   102\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   103\t\n   104\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   105\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   106\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   107\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   108\t\n   109\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   110\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   111\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   112\t\n   113\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   114\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   115\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   116\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   117\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   118\t            else:\n   119\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   120\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   121\t\n   122\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   123\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   124\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   125\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   126\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   127\t\n   128\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   129\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   130\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   131\t        else:\n   132\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   133\t\n   134\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   135\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   136\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   137\t        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\n   138\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   139\t        epsilon = float(np.exp(log_eps))\n   140\t\n   141\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   142\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   143\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   144\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   145\t            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   146\t\n   147\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   148\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"nl -ba /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py | sed -n '1,280p' && echo '---PARAM-NL---' && nl -ba /hada/metabbo/param_controller.py | sed -n '1,260p'\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"1\t\"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer for cocoex problems.\n     2\t\n     3\tThis module provides a `DEOptimizer` class that is controlled by a DQN agent\n     4\tfor hyperparameter optimization. The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\n     5\tobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\n     6\tadjustment (F, CR).\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\t\n     9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = None\n    16\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    17\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    18\t\n    19\t\n    20\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n    42\t                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n    43\t                 swarm_size: int = None):\n    44\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t        selected = []\n    71\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    72\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    73\t            selected.append(idx)\n    74\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    75\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    76\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    77\t                best_idx = idx\n    78\t        return best_idx\n    79\t\n    80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n    83\t\n    84\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    85\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    86\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    87\t        \n    88\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    89\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    90\t\n    91\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n    92\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n    93\t        \n    94\t        # Evaluate initial population\n    95\t        evals = 0\n    96\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n    97\t        \n    98\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n    99\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   100\t                break\n   101\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   102\t            evals += 1\n   103\t\n   104\t        # Track best\n   105\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   106\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   107\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   108\t        \n   109\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   110\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   111\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   112\t\n   113\t        # ---- SHADE (Success-History based Adaptive DE) ----\n   114\t        # Memory of successful F and CR values\n   115\t        H = 6\n   116\t        MF = np.full(H, 0.5)\n   117\t        MCR = np.full(H, 0.5)\n   118\t        k_mem = 0\n   119\t\n   120\t        # External archive of replaced individuals\n   121\t        archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n   122\t        archive_cap = max(1, pop_size)\n   123\t\n   124\t        # Main loop\n   125\t        iteration = 0\n   126\t        done = False\n   127\t        \n   128\t        while not done:\n   129\t            iteration += 1\n   130\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   131\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   132\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   133\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   134\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   135\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   136\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   137\t            \n   138\t            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   139\t            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   140\t            \n   141\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   142\t\n   143\t            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n   144\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   145\t            pbest_num = max(1, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n   146\t\n   147\t            # Per-individual parameters and success records\n   148\t            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   149\t            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   150\t            S_F = []\n   151\t            S_CR = []\n   152\t            S_delta = []\n   153\t\n   154\t            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n   155\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   156\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   157\t                # Sample F and CR from SHADE memory distributions\n   158\t                mi = int(rng.randint(H))\n   159\t                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n   160\t                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n   161\t                    F = base_F\n   162\t                F_vals[i] = float(np.clip(F, 0.05, 1.0))\n   163\t\n   164\t                CR = float(np.clip(rng.normal(MCR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n   165\t                if not np.isfinite(CR):\n   166\t                    CR = base_CR\n   167\t                CR_vals[i] = float(np.clip(CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n   168\t\n   169\t                # Choose pbest (best 20% individuals)\n   170\t                pi = sorted_idx[rng.randint(pbest_num)]\n   171\t\n   172\t                # Choose r1 from population excluding current i\n   173\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   174\t                if len(candidates) < 1:\n   175\t                    continue\n   176\t                r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n   177\t\n   178\t                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n   179\t                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < 0.5\n   180\t                if use_archive:\n   181\t                    idx2 = int(rng.randint(len(archive)))\n   182\t                    x_r2 = archive[idx2]\n   183\t                else:\n   184\t                    # ensure r2 distinct from i and r1 when selecting from population\n   185\t                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n   186\t                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n   187\t                        continue\n   188\t                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n   189\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   190\t\n   191\t                xi = pop[i]\n   192\t                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n   193\t                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n   194\t\n   195\t                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n   196\t                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n   197\t\n   198\t                # Binomial crossover\n   199\t                u = np.copy(xi)\n   200\t                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n   201\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   202\t                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n   203\t                        u[j] = v[j]\n   204\t\n   205\t                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n   206\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   207\t                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n   208\t                        u[j] = lb[j] + rng.rand() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n   209\t\n   210\t                new_pop[i] = u\n   211\t\n   212\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   213\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   214\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   215\t                    done = True\n   216\t                    break\n   217\t                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n   218\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   219\t                evals += 1\n   220\t                \n   221\t                # Greedy selection with archive update\n   222\t                if trial_f < old_f:\n   223\t                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n   224\t                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n   225\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n   226\t                    else:\n   227\t                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n   228\t                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n   229\t\n   230\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   231\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   232\t\n   233\t                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n   234\t                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n   235\t                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n   236\t                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n   237\t\n   238\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   239\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   240\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   241\t\n   242\t            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n   243\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   244\t                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n   245\t                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n   246\t                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n   247\t                w = w / np.sum(w)\n   248\t\n   249\t                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n   250\t                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n   251\t                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n   252\t                if den > 1e-12:\n   253\t                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n   254\t                else:\n   255\t                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n   256\t\n   257\t                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n   258\t                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n   259\t\n   260\t                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n   261\t\n   262\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   263\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   264\t            \n   265\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   266\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   267\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   268\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   269\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   270\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   271\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   272\t            \n   273\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   274\t\n   275\t        self.stats = {\n   276\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   277\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   278\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   279\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   280\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n---PARAM-NL---\n     1\t\"\"\"Parameter controller interface and simple implementations.\n     2\t\n     3\tDefines `ParamController` abstract interface and example controllers that can\n     4\tbe injected into `PSOOptimizer` to adjust parameters dynamically. This is\n     5\tthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\tfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\n     8\tfrom typing import Dict, Any\n     9\t\n    10\t# Import global configuration\n    11\timport sys\n    12\timport os\n    13\ttry:\n    14\t    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    15\t    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    16\t    # Set global seed at module load time\n    17\t    set_global_seed()\n    18\texcept Exception:\n    19\t    # Fallback if config module is not available\n    20\t    SEED = 42\n    21\t    def set_global_seed(*args, Main loop\n   132\t        iteration = 0\n   133\t        done = False\n   134\t        \n   135\t        while evals < main_budget and not done:\n   136\t            iteration += 1\n   137\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   138\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   139\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   140\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   141\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   142\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   143\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   144\t            \n   145\t            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   146\t            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   147\t            \n   148\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   149\t\n   150\t            # Sort indices by fitness to support pbest selection\n   151\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   152\t            pbest_num = max(1, int(0.2 * pop_size))\n   153\t\n   154\t            # Per-individual parameters and success records\n   155\t            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   156\t            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   157\t            S_F = []\n   158\t            S_CR = []\n   159\t            S_delta = []\n   160\t\n   161\t            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1 with archive\n   162\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   163\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   164\t                # Sample F and CR from SHADE memory distributions\n   165\t                mi = int(rng.randint(H))\n   166\t                F = float(np.clip(rng.standard_cauchy() * 0.1 + MF[mi], 0.0, 1.0))\n   167\t                if not np.isfinite(F) or F <= 0.0:\n   168\t                    F = base_F\n   169\t                F_vals[i] = float(np.clip(F, 0.05, 1.0))\n   170\t\n   171\t                CR = float(np.clip(rng.normal(MCR[mi], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\n   172\t                if not np.isfinite(CR):\n   173\t                    CR = base_CR\n   174\t                CR_vals[i] = float(np.clip(CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n   175\t\n   176\t                # Choose pbest (best 20% individuals)\n   177\t                pi = sorted_idx[rng.randint(pbest_num)]\n   178\t\n   179\t                # Choose r1 from population excluding current i\n   180\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   181\t                if len(candidates) < 1:\n   182\t                    continue\n   183\t                r1 = int(rng.choice(candidates))\n   184\t\n   185\t                # Choose r2 from archive (with probability) or from population\n   186\t                use_archive = len(archive) > 0 and rng.rand() < 0.5\n   187\t                if use_archive:\n   188\t                    idx2 = int(rng.randint(len(archive)))\n   189\t                    x_r2 = archive[idx2]\n   190\t                else:\n   191\t                    # ensure r2 distinct from i and r1 when selecting from population\n   192\t                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != r1]\n   193\t                    if len(candidates2) < 1:\n   194\t                        continue\n   195\t                    r2 = int(rng.choice(candidates2))\n   196\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n   197\t\n   198\t                xi = pop[i]\n   199\t                x_pbest = pop[pi]\n   200\t                x_r1 = pop[r1]\n   201\t\n   202\t                # current-to-pbest/1 with optional archive\n   203\t                v = xi + F_vals[i] * (x_pbest - xi) + F_vals[i] * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n   204\t\n   205\t                # Binomial crossover\n   206\t                u = np.copy(xi)\n   207\t                j_rand = rng.randint(self.dim)\n   208\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   209\t                    if rng.rand() < CR_vals[i] or j == j_rand:\n   210\t                        u[j] = v[j]\n   211\t\n   212\t                # Boundary handling: random reinitialization (better than clipping for multimodal problems)\n   213\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   214\t                    if u[j] < lb[j] or u[j] > ub[j]:\n   215\t                        u[j] = lb[j] + rng.rand() * (ub[j] - lb[j])\n   216\t\n   217\t                new_pop[i] = u\n   218\t\n   219\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   220\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   221\t                if evals >= main_budget:\n   222\t                    done = True\n   223\t                    break\n   224\t                old_f = float(fitness[i])\n   225\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   226\t                evals += 1\n   227\t                \n   228\t                # Greedy selection with archive update\n   229\t                if trial_f < old_f:\n   230\t                    # Add replaced individual to archive\n   231\t                    if len(archive) < archive_cap:\n   232\t                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i]])\n   233\t                    else:\n   234\t                        ar = rng.randint(archive_cap)\n   235\t                        archive[ar] = pop[i]\n   236\t\n   237\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   238\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   239\t\n   240\t                    # Record successful parameters for memory update\n   241\t                    S_F.append(F_vals[i])\n   242\t                    S_CR.append(CR_vals[i])\n   243\t                    S_delta.append(max(1e-30, abs(old_f - trial_f)))\n   244\t\n   245\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   246\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   247\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   248\t\n   249\t            # Update SHADE success-history memories\n   250\t            if len(S_F) > 0:\n   251\t                s_f = np.asarray(S_F)\n   252\t                s_cr = np.asarray(S_CR)\n   253\t                w = np.asarray(S_delta, dtype=float)\n   254\t                w = w / np.sum(w)\n   255\t\n   256\t                # Weighted Lehmer mean for F\n   257\t                num = float(np.sum(w * s_f * s_f))\n   258\t                den = float(np.sum(w * s_f))\n   259\t                if den > 1e-12:\n   260\t                    MF[k_mem] = num / den\n   261\t                else:\n   262\t                    MF[k_mem] = 0.5\n   263\t\n   264\t                # Weighted arithmetic mean for CR\n   265\t                MCR[k_mem] = float(np.sum(w * s_cr))\n   266\t\n   267\t                k_mem = (k_mem + 1) % H\n   268\t\n   269\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   270\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   271\t            \n   272\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   273\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   274\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   275\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   276\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   277\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   278\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   279\t            \n   280\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   281\t\n   282\t        # ---- Final local search: coordinate descent around best ----\n   283\t        # Use the reserved evaluation budget to refine gbest with axis-aligned\n   284\t        # line search / shrinking steps. This is highly effective for smooth\n   285\t        # unimodal functions and does not hurt multimodal ones because it is\n   286\t        # only a small fraction of the total budget and starts from the best.\n   287\t        step = 0.1 * (ub - lb)\n   288\t        local_evals = 0\n   289\t        while local_evals < local_budget and evals < self.max_evals:\n   290\t            improved_local = False\n   291\t            # Deterministic cyclic coordinate search\n   292\t            for j in range(self.dim):\n   293\t                if local_evals >= local_budget or evals >= self.max_evals:\n   294\t                    break\n   295\t                # Try positive and negative step with shrinking\n   296\t                for direction in (1.0, -1.0):\n   297\t                    if local_evals >= local_budget or evals >= self.max_evals:\n   298\t                        break\n   299\t                    trial = gbest.copy()\n   300\t                    trial[j] = np.clip(gbest[j] + direction * step[j], lb[j], ub[j])\n   301\t                    trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n   302\t                    evals += 1\n   303\t                    local_evals += 1\n   304\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   305\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   306\t                        gbest = trial.copy()\n   307\t                        improved_local = True\n   308\t                        # Increase step slightly if moving consistently\n   309\t                        step[j] *= 1.2\n   310\t                        break\n   311\t            if not improved_local:\n   312\t                # Shrink the step size to refine precision\n   313\t                step *= 0.8\n   314\t                if np.max(step) < 1e-12:\n   315\t                    break\n   316\t\n   317\t        self.stats = {\n   318\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   319\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   320\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   321\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   322\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   323\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   324\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   325\t        }\n   326\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

In ec_algorithm.py, I reserved 30% of the evaluation budget for a final coordinate-descent local refinement around the best solution, which is especially effective on smooth/unimodal BBOB functions like f1. The main DE loop now stops at the reserved main budget and then performs adaptive cyclic coordinate search with step growing/shrinking to polish gbest. I also updated both DummyHeuristicController and AdaptiveDEController in param_controller.py to schedule F/CR/w/c1/c2 based on normalized evaluation budget (evals/max_evals) rather than raw iteration count, giving smoother budget-aware exploration-to-exploitation transitions.\"\n}",
